- **pei_duo_fei**: - **persona_id**: `pei_duo_fei` - **姓名**: 裴多菲·山陀尔（Sándor Petőfi） - **原文名**: Petőfi Sándor（匈牙利语顺序） - **生卒**: 1823年1月1日 – 1849年7月31日，享年26岁 - **出生地**: 匈牙利基斯凯雷什（Kiskőrös），奥地利帝国 - **逝世地**: 特兰西瓦尼亚塞盖什瓦尔（Segesvár），奥地利帝国（今罗马尼亚锡吉什瓦拉） - **死因**: 塞盖什瓦尔战役中失踪，据信战死，遗体从未被发现 - **语言**: 匈牙利语 ([111pointer111/claude-marketplace](https://github.com/111pointer111/claude-marketplace/tree/HEAD/skills/historical-persona-distiller/output/pei_duo_fei) · ★4)
- **pei_gen**: **原文引用：** - "human knowledge and human power come to the same thing" ——《新工具》Novum Organum I:3 - "the Soueraignetie of man lyeth hidd in knowledge, wherein many things are reserued which kings with their treasures cannot buy nor with theire forces Commande" ——《论学术的推进》The Advancement of Learning - "I have taken all knowledge to be my province" ——1591年致伯利勋爵信 ([111pointer111/claude-marketplace](https://github.com/111pointer111/claude-marketplace/tree/HEAD/skills/historical-persona-distiller/output/pei_gen) · ★4)

- **peijue-hongtuo**: 用户在创作短剧/网文，需要"配角烘托"类桥段：所有人预判主角必败主角却完胜、反派引以为傲的靠山是主角手下、徒弟狂妄但师傅敬畏主角、主角的粉丝/手下主动出手替他摆平麻烦时调用。 用于生成"主角不出手也显强"的地位型爽点、设计多视角烘托结构。不适用于：主角亲自上阵的战斗戏（那是 b01/b03）；无配角体系的独角戏。 触发信号：配角烘托、所有人都不看好、反派靠山是主角手下、粉丝代劳、主角不出手（indirect power display）。 ([icooldp/400-duanju-shuangdian](https://github.com/icooldp/400-duanju-shuangdian/tree/HEAD/b-shili-nianya/b06-peijue-hongtuo) · ★14)

- **peitho**: The go-to-market god. Use PEITHO to position, price, and sell anything you have built: the wedge, the offer, the landing page, the launch post, the ads, the pitch, the cold outreach. It refuses to write a headline before the positioning exists, rejects any line a competitor could have written unchanged, and makes every angle carry the cheapest test that would settle it. Trigger on "position this", "write the landing page", "how do I launch this", "price this", "/peitho". Not for deciding WHETHER to enter a market (that is ATHENA), and it never grades its own pitch (that is ZOILUS). ([eragonlonelyboy-lab/peitho](https://github.com/eragonlonelyboy-lab/peitho/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))
- **peitho**: Use when writing or revising any prose longer than a few sentences, in any form and any medium: article, blog post, marketing copy, landing page, README, email, chapter, story, social post, release notes, or the prose parts of a spec. - **peiyin**: 给视频配音：把口播稿、旁白、解说词、台词念成音频。默认用本机的 VoxCPM2（mlx-audio，Apple Silicon，不花钱，稿子不出电脑）：每段生成后过声音门禁（嗡声、底噪、削波、语速、Whisper 对稿），不过就换种子重来，整条拼好再过一次门禁；出每段音频、整条音轨、对得上的 SRT 字幕和每段时长表（给剪辑和 Remotion 对时间用），改稿后只重做改过的段。还能用一句话描述造一个声音，或者用自己的录音克隆声音（先降噪清洗，再试念过门禁才入库）。MiniMax 云端合成作为可选后端保留。用户说「配音」「旁白」「念出来」「TTS」「语音合成」「给这条视频配个音」「用我的声音念」「出一条人声轨」「配音加字幕」「造个声音」「声音克隆」「VoxCPM」「MiniMax 配音」时使用。已经有配音、要给配音配画面动效的用 video-talkcraft；只要背景音乐或音效的用 eagle-sfx。 ([MatsuriMW/aigc-skills-and-agents](https://github.com/MatsuriMW/aigc-skills-and-agents/tree/HEAD/skills/peiyin))

- **pelizzai-merge-recovery**: Use when a git merge or rebase conflict is in progress. Preserves the intent of both sides; never aborts on its own — an unresolvable conflict escalates to the user. ([rpelizza/PelizzAI](https://github.com/rpelizza/PelizzAI/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/pelizzai-merge-recovery) · ★3 · 4 copies)
- **pelizzai-module-design**: Shared vocabulary for deep modules. Use when designing or improving a module's interface, deciding where a seam goes, or making code more testable and navigable. ([rpelizza/PelizzAI](https://github.com/rpelizza/PelizzAI/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/pelizzai-module-design) · ★3 · 4 copies)
- **pelizzai-onboard**: Initializes PelizzAI in a project or workspace. Invoke on the user's first contact with the harness, or on 'bootstrap', 'remap', 'onboard'. Scans first; writes only after ratification. ([rpelizza/PelizzAI](https://github.com/rpelizza/PelizzAI/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/pelizzai-onboard) · ★3 · 4 copies)
- **pelizzai-oswap**: Security overlay for changes that touch authentication/authorization, untrusted input, SQL/queries, sensitive data, uploads, CORS/SSRF, dependencies/supply chain, integrity, logging, or exceptions. Applies the current OWASP Top 10 to the diff and produces fixes/evidence before the final review and validated-head. Use also when the user asks for an OWASP review; never defer it to finish-task. ([renatosantos0611/OpenTimbre](https://github.com/renatosantos0611/OpenTimbre/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/pelizzai-oswap) · 2 copies)
